I haven't confirmed it's the exact action in the recording, because `/app/.harbor_assets/` is empty and `recording.mp4` isn't anywhere on the system. I worked from your written description and the code. The project builds; I didn't run the game.\n\n**What was wrong:** The bug is in the collision response in `src/Space.cpp` (`hitCallback`, the branch for a ship hitting a station, which doesn't move). The direction of a contact depends on which object reported it. When the station's edges are the ones touching the ship's surface, the direction points the wrong way, so the code has to flip it. It already computed a corrected direction (`hitNormal`) but never used it. The speed check, the bounce force and the push-out all used the unflipped direction. For those contacts:\n- A ship moving **away** from the station counted as an impact. It took damage straight away, before visibly hitting anything. A ship resting on a pad that then lifts off, after the station has released it, falls into this case.\n- The bounce pushed the ship back **into** the surface, so the action didn't go as intended.\n- A ship actually approaching a surface was ignored for those same contacts.\n\nWhether a contact is reported in that order depends only on the shapes touching, not on a particular pad. That fits the bug showing up at every equivalent spot on a station.\n\n**Fix:** Four lines in that branch now use the corrected direction: the speed check, the moment-of-inertia term, the bounce force and the push-out. Ship-to-ship collisions and the station's docking and damage rules are untouched.\n\n**One thing to check if damage still shows up:** If the recording shows a manual launch from a rotating orbital station, the code that lifts the ship off the pad uses very small margins. It moves the ship 1 cm off the pad and gives it 0.05 m/s of upward speed (`Player::DoFixspeedTakeoff` in `src/Player.cpp`). I left these alone because nothing showed they were the cause. If damage persists in that case, they're where I'd look next."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":438,"cache_read_input_tokens":62951,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":438,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7bb77686-6321-459b-b10b-7049b9e70f5a","uuid":"58b7098a-3cc3-4c48-b054-606966f11379","timestamp":"2026-11-15T20:09:03.169Z"}
